# META
~~~ini
description=A connection definition body is a DefinitionBody (SysML BNF 664, 234), so action, state, exhibit state, constraint, calc and perform members lower as members of the connection definition, as they do in a part definition body; a connection usage keeps a multiplicity written after its redefinition (`connection mainLines :>> lines[3]`)
type=file
libraries=standard
require_no_diagnostics=true
require_complete_publication=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Grid {
    private import ScalarValues::*;

    part def Bus;
    action def Sense;
    action def Report;
    constraint def Limit;
    calc def Ratio { return : Real; }

    connection def Line {
        end a : Bus;
        end b : Bus;
        attribute temperature : Real;
        action sense : Sense;
        state def Mode;
        exhibit state protection {
            entry; then closed;
            state closed;
            state open;
            transition overheat first closed then open;
        }
        constraint limit : Limit;
        calc def LocalRatio { return : Real; }
        calc ratio : Ratio;
        perform action report : Report;
    }

    part def Network {
        part buses : Bus[0..*];
        connection lines : Line[0..*];
    }

    part network : Network {
        connection mainLines :>> lines[3];
    }
}
~~~
